/* frontend/metrics-experience.css */
/* CP-CRM-METRIC-01 — global signed metrics presentation.
 * Preserve exact canonical text, chart contracts and content visibility.
 * Decorative SVG/Canvas gauges only when a rendered value is a valid 0–100%.
 * All animation state is scoped, reduced-motion aware, and CSP compliant.
 */
:root{
 --aps-metric-accent:#6879db;
 --aps-metric-track:#dae4f6;
 --aps-metric-outline:#e1e8f6;
 --aps-metric-ease:cubic-bezier(.2,.75,.25,1);
}
#app .aps-metric-card{
 position:relative;
 isolation:isolate;
 min-width:0;
}
#app .aps-metric-value{
 font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;
 font-feature-settings:"tnum" 1;
}

 outline-offset:2px;
}

 box-sizing:border-box;
 position:absolute;

 width:52px;height:52px;
 max-width:22%;max-height:65%;
 display:block;overflow:visible;
 opacity:.70;pointer-events:none;
 z-index:0;flex:none;
}

 stroke-linecap:round;
}
#app .aps-metric-gauge-canvas{

 image-rendering:auto;
}

}

}

/* frontend/platform-sync.css */
/* CP-CRM-RT01 · Precision Orbital Sync Pulse.
 * Native-compositor microanimation only during actual connecting or syncing.
 * Idle and hidden indicators consume no animation frames.
 */
#app .aps-sync{
 display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;
 min-height:31px;max-width:158px;padding:5px 10px 5px 8px;
 border:1px solid #dfe6f7;border-radius:999px;background:#f7f9ff;
 color:#52658a;font-size:10px;font-weight:750;line-height:1.1;
 cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;flex:none;letter-spacing:.01em;
 transition:border-color .15s ease,background-color .15s ease,color .15s ease;
}
#app .aps-sync:hover{background:#edf2ff;border-color:#b6c4ef}
#app .aps-sync:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #5965dd;outline-offset:2px}
#app .aps-sync-caption{overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;min-width:0}
#app .aps-sync-orbit{
 width:16px;height:16px;flex:none;border-radius:50%;
 display:inline-block;position:relative;
 background:conic-gradient(from 25deg,#5966ea 0 60deg,#98d6ff 60deg 118deg,transparent 118deg 290deg,#5966ea 290deg 360deg);
 -webkit-mask:radial-gradient(farthest-side,transparent 63%,#000 68%);
 mask:radial-gradient(farthest-side,transparent 63%,#000 68%);
}
#app .aps-sync-orbit::after{
 content:"";position:absolute;inset:5px;border-radius:50%;
 background:#7b96ee;opacity:.65;
}
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="synchronized"]{
 color:#187258;border-color:#c9e9dc;background:#f0fbf5;
}
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="synchronized"] .aps-sync-orbit{
 background:conic-gradient(from 25deg,#22a67e 0 320deg,transparent 320deg);
 animation:none;
}
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="connecting"] .aps-sync-orbit,
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="synchronizing"] .aps-sync-orbit{
 animation:aps-sync-orbit 1.2s linear infinite;
}
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="reconnecting"]{
 color:#987122;border-color:#f0ddb1;background:#fffaf0;
}
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="reconnecting"] .aps-sync-orbit{
 background:conic-gradient(#db9b3e,transparent 72%,#db9b3e);
 animation:aps-sync-orbit 1.65s ease-in-out infinite;
}
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="offline"],
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="error"]{
 color:#aa5160;border-color:#f1cad2;background:#fff4f6;
}
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="offline"] .aps-sync-orbit,
#app .aps-sync[data-sync-state="error"] .aps-sync-orbit{
 background:conic-gradient(#c45e70 0 180deg,transparent 180deg);
 animation:none;
}
@keyframes aps-sync-orbit{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
@media(max-width:570px){
 #app .top-actions .aps-sync{padding:6px;min-width:29px;max-width:29px}

}

}

/* frontend/foreground-overlay.css */
/* CP-CRM-OVERLAY-02 — reliable implicit popover anchoring.
 * showPopover({source:trigger}) supplies the implicit anchor. With anchor()
 * offsets, position-anchor:auto MUST be explicit (not a named anchor).
 * Works for overview-card menus outside .aps-board-shell as well as table cells.
 * Top-layer paint escapes clipped scroll/table ancestors; no z-index inflation.
 */
@supports (position-anchor:auto) and (top:anchor(bottom)){
 .aps-foreground-surface[popover]:popover-open{
  box-sizing:border-box;
  position:fixed!important;
  position-anchor:auto!important;
  inset:auto!important;
  margin:0!important;
  max-height:calc(100dvh - 18px);
  max-width:calc(100vw - 18px);
  overflow-y:auto;
  overscroll-behavior:contain;
  position-try-fallbacks:flip-block,flip-inline;
 }
 /* Keep an unresolved anchor from ever flashing at 0,0. The component
    removes this flag after its first verified layout measurement. */
 .aps-foreground-surface.aps-foreground-pending:popover-open{
  visibility:hidden!important;
 }
 .aps-select-listbox.aps-foreground-select:popover-open{
  top:calc(anchor(bottom) + 7px)!important;
  left:anchor(left)!important;
  right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;
  width:anchor-size(width);min-width:140px;
  max-width:min(480px,calc(100vw - 18px));
  max-height:min(360px,calc(100dvh - 18px));
 }
 .aps-select-listbox.aps-foreground-select.aps-select-above:popover-open{
  top:auto!important;bottom:calc(anchor(top) + 7px)!important;
 }
 .aps-select-listbox.aps-foreground-select.aps-select-end:popover-open{
  left:auto!important;right:anchor(right)!important;
 }
 /* Unscoped coverage is necessary: Overview board cards are not members
    of the .aps-board-shell subtree. */
 .aps-action-popover.aps-foreground-action:popover-open{
  top:calc(anchor(bottom) + 8px)!important;
  right:anchor(right)!important;
  left:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;
  width:max-content;min-width:210px;
  max-width:min(310px,calc(100vw - 20px));
  max-height:min(370px,calc(100dvh - 20px));
 }
 .aps-action-popover.aps-foreground-action.aps-menu-above:popover-open{
  top:auto!important;bottom:calc(anchor(top) + 8px)!important;
 }
 .aps-action-popover.aps-foreground-action.aps-menu-align-start:popover-open{
  right:auto!important;left:anchor(left)!important;
 }
 .aps-search-suggestions.aps-foreground-search:popover-open{
  top:calc(anchor(bottom) + 8px)!important;
  left:anchor(left)!important;
  right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;
  width:anchor-size(width);min-width:210px;
  max-width:min(420px,calc(100vw - 18px));
  max-height:min(315px,calc(100dvh - 18px));
 }
 .aps-search-suggestions.aps-foreground-search.aps-search-above:popover-open{
  top:auto!important;bottom:calc(anchor(top) + 8px)!important;
 }
 .aps-search-suggestions.aps-foreground-search.aps-search-align-end:popover-open{
  left:auto!important;right:anchor(right)!important;
 }
}
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
 .aps-foreground-surface[popover]:popover-open{
  animation:none!important;transition:none!important;
 }
}

/* frontend/soft-minimalist.css */
/* APSYNTRA CRM | Soft Minimalist Enterprise Design System
 * Visual-only. This file loads after existing route styles to reconcile
 * established components without changing routing, permissions or behavior.
 * New refinements must reuse these tokens rather than recreate palettes.
 */

  --sm-danger-soft:#FBEFF1;

/* BS03 — contextual drag surfaces and accessible column actions.
 * Columns are dragged directly by their headers; tabs are dragged by their labels.
 * The contextual three-dot and resize controls are independent of dragging.
 */

  position:absolute;
  top:50%;
  transform:translateY(-50%);
  float:none;
  display:inline-flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:center;
  box-sizing:border-box;
  padding:0;
  margin:0;
  background:transparent;
  color:#7785a0;
  border:1px solid transparent;
  border-radius:7px;
  box-shadow:none;
  font-size:13px;
  line-height:1;
  z-index:5;
}

  right:0;width:22px;min-width:22px;height:29px;min-height:29px;
  cursor:col-resize;touch-action:none;
}

/* Canonical route selection: only the aria-current link receives active treatment.
   Keep normal links quiet; pointer hover and keyboard focus remain independent. */

 background:transparent;

 box-shadow:none;
 color:var(--sm-sub);
 font-weight:620;
}

 opacity:0;transform:translateY(-50%) scaleY(.55);
}
